# Symbol usage

Generated by `cargo xtask usage-scan` on 2026-10-07. Don't edit it by hand; rerun the scan to refresh it
(it downloads about 5 GB, and caches what it read).

Which rustssl exports real software calls. Every amd64 package in these distributions that depends
on OpenSSL's libraries was downloaded, and the imports of its ELF files were read (never run). A
package counts once per function, however many of its files import it. Breadth phases implement
functions in this order (IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md, step 0.10).

| Distribution | OpenSSL | Packages | Scanned | Importing OpenSSL |
|---|---|--:|--:|--:|
| Debian 13 (trixie) | 3.5.7-1~deb13u2 | 917 | 917 | 903 |
| Ubuntu 26.04 LTS (resolute) | 3.5.5-1ubuntu3 | 1007 | 1007 | 990 |
| Fedora 44 | 3.5.5-1.fc44 | 996 | 996 | 921 |

*Importing OpenSSL* counts the packages with an ELF file that imports from libssl or libcrypto. The
others depend on the libraries without calling them directly: through another library, or from
scripts.

N most-imported ones:

| Top N | Debian | Ubuntu | Fedora | All |
|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| 10 | 2 of 903 | 1 of 990 | 0 of 921 | 3 of 2814 |

| 50 | 19 of 903 | 21 of 990 | 17 of 921 | 57 of 2814 |
| 100 | 33 of 903 | 40 of 990 | 35 of 921 | 108 of 2814 |

| 500 | 193 of 903 | 208 of 990 | 194 of 921 | 595 of 2814 |
| 1000 | 383 of 903 | 419 of 990 | 343 of 921 | 1145 of 2814 |
| 2000 | 612 of 903 | 688 of 990 | 610 of 921 | 1910 of 2814 |
| 3957 (all imported) | 903 of 903 | 990 of 990 | 914 of 921 | 2807 of 2814 |

## Imports rustssl doesn't export

These imports name a function or version node that isn't in the OpenSSL 3.5 export list rustssl
follows. The packages that use them won't load until it's resolved.

| Import | Library | Packages |
|---|---|---|
| `BIO_new_dgram_sctp@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora openssl |
| `EVP_CIPHER_CTX_dup@OPENSSL_3.1.0` | libcrypto | Fedora dpdk |
| `EVP_MD_CTX_dup@OPENSSL_3.1.0` | libcrypto | Fedora dpdk |
| `EVP_md2@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora qca-qt5-ossl, Fedora qca-qt6-ossl |
| `MD2@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora perl-Net-SSLeay |
| `MD2_Final@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora ipmievd, Fedora ipmitool |
| `MD2_Init@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora ipmievd, Fedora ipmitool |
| `MD2_Update@OPENSSL_3.0.0` | libcrypto | Fedora ipmievd, Fedora ipmitool |
